# Phase 5 (GOAL.md): functions whose definition is hand-written C++ in game/ instead of the recompiled
# body. Input of `p3fes_recomp --out ... --replace game/replaced.txt` (recomp/README.md "Replacements"):
# the emitter keeps the recompiled body under fn_<name>_<addr>__recomp (for the differential test in
# tests/game/) and leaves the plain symbol, which game/src/ defines, to the hand-written version.
# Format: '<hex start> <IDA name as in symbols/functions.json>' per line; '#' starts a comment.
# A name that no longer matches functions.json stops the emitter (stale list after an IDA rename).
#
